31:Tc582,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> BoundNames takes no arguments and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings.</p>
      <emu-note id="note-star-default-star"><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p><emu-val>"\</emu-val>default\<emu-val>"</emu-val> is used within this specification as a synthetic name for a module's default export when it does not have another name. An entry in the module's <var class="field">[[Environment]]</var> is created with that name and holds the corresponding value, and resolving the export named <emu-val>"default"</emu-val> by calling <emu-xref href="#sec-resolveexport" title><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-resolveexport">ResolveExport ( <var>exportName</var> [ , <var>resolveSet</var> ] )</a></emu-xref> for the module will return a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#resolvedbinding-record">ResolvedBinding Record</a></emu-xref> whose <var class="field">[[BindingName]]</var> is <emu-val>"\</emu-val>default\<emu-val>"</emu-val>, which will then resolve in the module's <var class="field">[[Environment]]</var> to the above-mentioned value. This is done only for ease of specification, so that anonymous default exports can be resolved like any other export. This <emu-val>"\</emu-val>default\<emu-val>"</emu-val> string is never accessible to ECMAScript code or to the module linking algorithm.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <p>It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-BindingIdentifier" name="BindingIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> whose sole element is the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-BindingIdentifier" name="BindingIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>yield</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>

      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-CaseBlock" name="CaseBlock" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseBlock">CaseBlock</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-DefaultClause">DefaultClause</a></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>If the first <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt> is present, let <var>names1</var> be the LexicallyDeclaredNames of the first <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Else, let <var>names1</var> be a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>Let <var>names2</var> be the LexicallyDeclaredNames of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-DefaultClause">DefaultClause</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>If the second <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt> is present, let <var>names3</var> be the LexicallyDeclaredNames of the second <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Else, let <var>names3</var> be a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li><li class="exit">Return the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation">list-concatenation</a></emu-xref> of <var>names1</var>, <var>names2</var>, and <var>names3</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

      <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>At the top level of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt>, function declarations are treated like var declarations rather than like lexical declarations.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The LexicallyDeclaredNames of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt> includes the names of all of its imported bindings.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ModuleItemList" name="ModuleItemList" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ModuleItemList">ModuleItemList</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ModuleItemList">ModuleItemList</a></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ModuleItem">ModuleItem</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>names1</var> be the LexicallyDeclaredNames of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ModuleItemList">ModuleItemList</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Let <var>names2</var> be the LexicallyDeclaredNames of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-ModuleItem">ModuleItem</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation">list-concatenation</a></emu-xref> of <var>names1</var> and <var>names2</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

      <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>At the top level of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>, function declarations are treated like lexical declarations rather than like var declarations.</p>

43:T6e2e,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> LexicallyScopedDeclarations takes no arguments and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Nodes</a></emu-xref>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-StatementList" name="StatementList" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementList">StatementList</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementList">StatementList</a></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementListItem">StatementListItem</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>decls1</var> be the LexicallyScopedDeclarations of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementList">StatementList</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Let <var>decls2</var> be the LexicallyScopedDeclarations of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementListItem">StatementListItem</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation">list-concatenation</a></emu-xref> of <var>decls1</var> and <var>decls2</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-StatementListItem" name="StatementListItem" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementListItem">StatementListItem</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-Statement">Statement</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-Statement">Statement</a></emu-nt> is <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-Statement">Statement</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-LabelledStatement">LabelledStatement</a></emu-nt></emu-grammar>, return the LexicallyScopedDeclarations of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-LabelledStatement">LabelledStatement</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-StatementListItem" name="StatementListItem" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementListItem">StatementListItem</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-Declaration">Declaration</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> whose sole element is the <emu-xref aoid="DeclarationPart"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-declarationpart">DeclarationPart</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-Declaration">Declaration</a></emu-nt>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-CaseBlock" name="CaseBlock" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseBlock">CaseBlock</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-CaseBlock" name="CaseBlock" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseBlock">CaseBlock</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-DefaultClause">DefaultClause</a></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>If the first <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt> is present, let <var>decls1</var> be the LexicallyScopedDeclarations of the first <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Else, let <var>decls1</var> be a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>Let <var>decls2</var> be the LexicallyScopedDeclarations of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-DefaultClause">DefaultClause</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>If the second <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt> is present, let <var>decls3</var> be the LexicallyScopedDeclarations of the second <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Else, let <var>decls3</var> be a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.</li><li class="exit">Return the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation">list-concatenation</a></emu-xref> of <var>decls1</var>, <var>decls2</var>, and <var>decls3</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-CaseClauses" name="CaseClauses" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClauses">CaseClauses</a></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-CaseClause">CaseClause</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>

52:T616b,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> Contains takes argument <var>symbol</var> (a grammar symbol) and returns a Boolean. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
      <p>Every grammar production alternative in this specification which is not listed below implicitly has the following default definition of Contains:</p>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>For each child node <var>child</var> of this <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>, do<ol><li class="exit">If <var>child</var> is an instance of <var>symbol</var>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li>If <var>child</var> is an instance of a nonterminal, then<ol><li>Let <var>contained</var> be the result of <var>child</var> Contains <var>symbol</var>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>contained</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li></ol></li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-FunctionDeclaration" name="FunctionDeclaration"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-FunctionExpression" name="FunctionExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionExpression">FunctionExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-GeneratorDeclaration" name="GeneratorDeclaration"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorDeclaration">GeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-GeneratorExpression" name="GeneratorExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration" name="AsyncGeneratorDeclaration"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorBody">AsyncGeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorBody">AsyncGeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression" name="AsyncGeneratorExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorBody">AsyncGeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration" name="AsyncFunctionDeclaration"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration">AsyncFunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-AsyncFunctionExpression" name="AsyncFunctionExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionExpression">AsyncFunctionExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Static semantic rules that depend upon substructure generally do not look into function definitions.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ClassTail" name="ClassTail" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassTail">ClassTail</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassHeritage">ClassHeritage</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassBody">ClassBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>symbol</var> is <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassBody">ClassBody</a></emu-nt>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li>If <var>symbol</var> is <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassHeritage">ClassHeritage</a></emu-nt>, then<ol><li class="exit">If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassHeritage">ClassHeritage</a></emu-nt> is present, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></li><li>If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassHeritage">ClassHeritage</a></emu-nt> is present, then<ol><li class="exit">If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassHeritage">ClassHeritage</a></emu-nt> Contains <var>symbol</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return the result of <emu-xref aoid="ComputedPropertyContains"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-computedpropertycontains">ComputedPropertyContains</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassBody">ClassBody</a></emu-nt> with argument <var>symbol</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Static semantic rules that depend upon substructure generally do not look into class bodies except for <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyName">PropertyName</a></emu-nt>s.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ClassStaticBlock" name="ClassStaticBlock" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassStaticBlock">ClassStaticBlock</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>static</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassStaticBlockBody">ClassStaticBlockBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Static semantic rules that depend upon substructure generally do not look into <code>static</code> initialization blocks.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ArrowFunction" name="ArrowFunction" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowParameters">ArrowParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>=></emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ConciseBody">ConciseBody</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>symbol</var> is not one of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-NewTarget">NewTarget</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperProperty">SuperProperty</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperCall">SuperCall</a></emu-nt>, <code>super</code>, or <code>this</code>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowParameters">ArrowParameters</a></emu-nt> Contains <var>symbol</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ConciseBody">ConciseBody</a></emu-nt> Contains <var>symbol</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ArrowParameters" name="ArrowParameters" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowParameters">ArrowParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>formals</var> be the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFormalParameters">ArrowFormalParameters</a></emu-nt> that is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">covered</a></emu-xref> by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return <var>formals</var> Contains <var>symbol</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-AsyncArrowFunction" name="AsyncArrowFunction" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowFunction">AsyncArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowBindingIdentifier">AsyncArrowBindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>=></emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncConciseBody">AsyncConciseBody</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>symbol</var> is not one of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-NewTarget">NewTarget</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperProperty">SuperProperty</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperCall">SuperCall</a></emu-nt>, <code>super</code>, or <code>this</code>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncConciseBody">AsyncConciseBody</a></emu-nt> Contains <var>symbol</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-AsyncArrowFunction" name="AsyncArrowFunction" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowFunction">AsyncArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-CoverCallExpressionAndAsyncArrowHead">CoverCallExpressionAndAsyncArrowHead</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>=></emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncConciseBody">AsyncConciseBody</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>symbol</var> is not one of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-NewTarget">NewTarget</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperProperty">SuperProperty</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperCall">SuperCall</a></emu-nt>, <code>super</code>, or <code>this</code>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li>Let <var>head</var> be the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowHead">AsyncArrowHead</a></emu-nt> that is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">covered</a></emu-xref> by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-CoverCallExpressionAndAsyncArrowHead">CoverCallExpressionAndAsyncArrowHead</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>head</var> Contains <var>symbol</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncConciseBody">AsyncConciseBody</a></emu-nt> Contains <var>symbol</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note data-num="4"><span class="note">Note 4</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Contains is used to detect <code>new.target</code>, <code>this</code>, and <code>super</code> usage within an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowFunction">AsyncArrowFunction</a></emu-nt>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-LiteralPropertyName" name="LiteralPropertyName" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-LiteralPropertyName">LiteralPropertyName</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-MemberExpression" name="MemberExpression" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>.</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> Contains <var>symbol</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

5a:T8f12,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> ContainsUndefinedContinueTarget takes arguments <var>iterationSet</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings) and <var>labelSet</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of Strings) and returns a Boolean. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>

5b:T64c4,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> IsFunctionDefinition takes no arguments and returns a Boolean. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-PrimaryExpression" name="PrimaryExpression" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PrimaryExpression">PrimaryExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>expr</var> be the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ParenthesizedExpression">ParenthesizedExpression</a></emu-nt> that is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">covered</a></emu-xref> by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return IsFunctionDefinition of <var>expr</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-PrimaryExpression" name="PrimaryExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PrimaryExpression">PrimaryExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>this</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-IdentifierReference">IdentifierReference</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Literal">Literal</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ArrayLiteral">ArrayLiteral</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ObjectLiteral">ObjectLiteral</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-RegularExpressionLiteral">RegularExpressionLiteral</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-TemplateLiteral">TemplateLiteral</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production>

60:T2f57,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> BindingInitialization takes arguments <var>value</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref>) and <var>envRecord</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">abrupt completion</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p><emu-val>undefined</emu-val> is passed for <var>envRecord</var> to indicate that a <emu-xref aoid="PutValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-putvalue">PutValue</a></emu-xref> operation should be used to assign the initialization value. This is the case for <code>var</code> statements and formal parameter lists of some <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function">non-strict functions</a></emu-xref> (See <emu-xref href="#sec-functiondeclarationinstantiation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-functiondeclarationinstantiation">10.2.11</a></emu-xref>). In those cases a lexical binding is hoisted and preinitialized prior to evaluation of its initializer.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <p>It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-BindingIdentifier" name="BindingIdentifier" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>name</var> be the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return ? <emu-xref aoid="InitializeBoundName"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-initializeboundname">InitializeBoundName</a></emu-xref>(<var>name</var>, <var>value</var>, <var>envRecord</var>).</li></ol></emu-alg>

      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ObjectBindingPattern" name="ObjectBindingPattern" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-ObjectBindingPattern">ObjectBindingPattern</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-const>unused</emu-const>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

      55:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-runtime-semantics-bindinginitialization","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"862-runtime-semantics-bindinginitialization","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"8.6.2"}]," Runtime Semantics: BindingInitialization"],["$","$L30",null,{"id":"862-runtime-semantics-bindinginitialization"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$60"}}],"$L61"]}]
62:T6f8c,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> IteratorBindingInitialization takes arguments <var>iteratorRecord</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-iterator-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-iterator-records">Iterator Record</a></emu-xref>) and <var>envRecord</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">abrupt completion</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>When <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> is passed for <var>envRecord</var> it indicates that a <emu-xref aoid="PutValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-putvalue">PutValue</a></emu-xref> operation should be used to assign the initialization value. This is the case for formal parameter lists of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function">non-strict functions</a></emu-xref>. In that case the formal parameter bindings are preinitialized in order to deal with the possibility of multiple parameters with the same name.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <p>It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>

63:T6e52,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> AssignmentTargetType takes no arguments and returns <emu-const>simple</emu-const>, <emu-const>web-compat</emu-const>, or <emu-const>invalid</emu-const>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-IdentifierReference" name="IdentifierReference" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-IdentifierReference">IdentifierReference</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>

64:T3533,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> PropName takes no arguments and returns a String or <emu-const>empty</emu-const>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-PropertyDefinition" name="PropertyDefinition" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinition">PropertyDefinition</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-IdentifierReference">IdentifierReference</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-IdentifierReference">IdentifierReference</a></emu-nt>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-PropertyDefinition" name="PropertyDefinition" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinition">PropertyDefinition</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-t>...</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-AssignmentExpression">AssignmentExpression</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-const>empty</emu-const>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-PropertyDefinition" name="PropertyDefinition" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyDefinition">PropertyDefinition</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-PropertyName">PropertyName</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>:</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-AssignmentExpression">AssignmentExpression</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
